# S5 + S9 complete diagnostic index

## Start here: all collective analysis and diagnostic files

**FINAL:16/16 completed, ledger130/200. All four roles are saved for every run.**

| Artifact | What it contains |
| --- | --- |
| [Final research decision](S5_S9_FINAL_RESEARCH_DECISION.md) | Concise four-family conclusion, controls, annual consistency and uncertainty |
| [Generated full research tables](S5_S9_GENERATED_RESEARCH_TABLES.md) | Preserved complete numerical tables behind the curated decision |
| [All model/control metrics](S5_S9_MODEL_METRICS.csv) | All64 neural roles plus same-task controls, counts and task-specific scores |
| [Matched baseline differences](S5_S9_MATCHED_BASELINE_DIFFERENCES.csv) | Role-level paired comparisons |
| [Annual/equal-year OOS uncertainty](S5_S9_PAIRED_OOS_UNCERTAINTY.csv) | Whole-month descriptive intervals and annual sign consistency |
| [Paired OOS session differences](S5_S9_OOS_PAIRED_SESSION_DIFFERENCES.csv) | Dependency-aware resampling input, shared stock cross-sections retained |
| [Detailed training and extreme-value report](S5_S9_DETAILED_TRAINING_AND_EXTREME_DIAGNOSTICS.md) | Calibration/validation/refit, exact optimized tail loss, NUM/MAD decomposition, complements and cases |
| [All64 role extreme summaries](S5_S9_EXTREME_LOSS_SUMMARY.csv) | Tail masses/loss shares, positive/negative, NUM-only/MAD-only/both/neither, floor and error tails |
| [Fixed-rule exact examples](S5_S9_FIXED_RULE_DIAGNOSTIC_EXAMPLES.csv) | Private local case rows, endpoints, labels/NUM/MAD/epsilon and source flags; event false alarms/misses and quantile misses |
| [Every epoch training trace](S5_S9_TRAINING_TRACE.csv) | Online dropout-ON versus fixed-checkpoint OFF losses, calibration and refit, raw-best/qualifying selection |
| [Training integrity summary](S5_S9_TRAINING_INTEGRITY_SUMMARY.csv) |89,187 update batches,535,122 nonzero gradient records, clipping and exact reload |
| [Volatility-conditional metrics](S5_S9_VOLATILITY_CONDITIONAL_METRICS.csv) | Proper loss, probability scores and quantile coverage using already-fitted prefix volatility cuts |
| [Stock/source/clock paired differences](S5_S9_OOS_PAIRED_SOURCE_STOCK_STRATA.csv) |1,764 matched OOS strata with support and original-weight mass |
| [Quantile median rank diagnostic](S5_S9_QUANTILE_MEDIAN_RANK_DIAGNOSTIC.csv) | Secondary signed median ranking, distinct from width/coverage |
| [Offline dashboard](S5_S9_VISUAL_DIAGNOSTICS.html) |16 genuine models ×4 roles ×13 panels; OOS full saved range, other trajectories first10 sessions with full-role summaries |
| [Run inventory](RUN_INVENTORY.csv) | All16 actual IDs and completion status |
| [Final verification](S5_S9_VERIFICATION.json) / [browser receipt](BROWSER_QA.json) | Numerical/source/privacy/budget/resources plus64 browser role checks and16 PNGs |
| [Independent stage checks](LIVE_STAGE_CHECKS.jsonl) | Hash verification of every completed per-run gate before final handoff |

### Every individual run

The remaining index lists **every file** in all16 actual run directories, including each `RUN_DIAGNOSTICS.md`. Within a run:

- `inner_train`, `inner_validation`, `refit`, `oos` `_scores.parquet`: exact private observations, true target/raw heads/inverses/losses, labels, NUM/MAD and source/recipe/checkpoint identities.
- Corresponding `_metrics.json`, `_strata.csv`, `_cohorts.csv`: all-role proper metrics, stock/month/slot/overnight/source/fallback/age groups and actual optimized tail contributions.
- S5 `ranking/`: reused complete S4 ranking, retrieval/margins/churn, common/centered score-energy, original-scale versus transformed-loss and additional cohort/verification files. Its indices/recipes preserve the original masks.
- Phase target/control JSON plus control-score Parquets: actual prefix-only recipes, thresholds, fitted controls and canonical-target bindings.
- `calibration_NNN.json`, `refit_NNN.json`, `_batches.csv`, `_gradients.csv`: complete epoch and optimizer evidence. `*_binding.json`/saved checkpoints/`configuration.json`/`PASS.json`: selected/raw-best/refit lineage, exact hyperparameters and file hashes.
- Root `plots/<run>.png`: compact static preview; dashboard `?view=<run>` opens that actual model.

### Frozen context and historical evidence

[S5 causal metadata dictionary](S5_CAUSAL_METADATA_MANIFEST.md) · [S9 historical targets/controls](S9_TARGET_AND_BASELINE_CONTRACT.md) · [Fold/role population census](S5_S9_ROLE_COUNTS.csv) · [Source/fallback coverage](S5_CAUSAL_COVERAGE_AND_DIAGNOSTIC_STRATA.csv) · [Sealed S4 recipes](S4_REUSED_RECIPES.json) · [Actual live approval](LIVE_AUTHORITY.json) · [Sealed design](design.json). The proposed wording on frozen manifests/cards is historical; the exact approved definitions were executed.

Old dashboards remain unchanged links: [S4](../hf20_s1s2_visual_s4a_2026-10-26_110310_preparation/plots/s4_a_rankings.html) · [S1/S2](../hf20_s1s2_visual_s4a_2026-10-26_110310_preparation/plots/historical_s1_s2_rankings.html).

Preparation drafts, the failed full-batch browser check, its HTML snapshot, the unextended generated page and untrained previews are preserved. They are historical evidence, **not the final dashboard or final PASS receipt**. Only the new per-run browser batches define final browser PASS. Private case/forecast/checkpoint/HTML files stay local and ignored. [Live session handoff](LIVE_WORK_SESSION_REPORT.md) · [Artifact inventory](artifact_inventory.csv).
